# Bus lines by town: province of Barcelona

Every bus line with at least one stop in each municipality of the province of Barcelona, from the ATM unified GTFS (see [README.md](README.md) for sources, method and caveats). ✅ = already in Arribo. Codes ending in `*` are the Generalitat's internal line number (the line has no public code). "Stops here" counts the line's distinct stops in that municipality.

Comarques: [Alt Penedès](#alt-penedès) · [Anoia](#anoia) · [Bages](#bages) · [Baix Llobregat](#baix-llobregat) · [Barcelonès](#barcelonès) · [Berguedà](#berguedà) · [Garraf](#garraf) · [Lluçanès](#lluçanès) · [Maresme](#maresme) · [Moianès](#moianès) · [Osona](#osona) · [Selva](#selva) · [Vallès Occidental](#vallès-occidental) · [Vallès Oriental](#vallès-oriental)
